# unitytk — Changelog

## 2026
- **2026-11-28 -- One vocabulary from the sheet to the build: the plan carries the sheet's words, cells and settings, and the Tasks kit builds one word per builder with its settings grouped by word (`unitytk/sheets/`: `readings/*.json`, `clauses/*.json`, `clause_spec.py`, `clause_book.py`, `reading_spec.py`, `_cell_reading.py`, `_row_reading.py`, `_plan_reading.py`, `_cell_writing.py`, `sheet_plan.py`, `sheet_check.py`, `delivery_index.py`; `Kits/Tasks/Editor/`; `Features/Manifests/Editor/` `ManifestValues`, `ManifestScene.Settings`, `ManifestBehaviorAttribute`, `ManifestBehaviors`; `Features/Indicators/Editor/IndicatorsActions.SetFlat` new; `Features/Placement` `SpotPlacementModel.Pointer`, `Placer.DefaultPointerLength`; `Samples~/Tasks`; `docs/tasks.md`, `docs/manifest.md`).** Breaking, with no aliases and no old name read: a plan is version 3 (a version 2 plan is refused: read its sheet again). Its `behavior` is the sheet's word (`highlight`, `indicate`, `show`, `hide`, `set`, `take`, `return`, `use`, `play`, `button`, `select`, `answer`, `travel`, `picture`, `list`, `tick`, `note`; the kit's own `advance`), its fields the cells by their names (`with`, `label`, `where` a list one per target; `restart` gone: `to` as written), its settings the sheet's as written; the reading's words table and every hide row are gone (a shown thing's span rides on its row). Clause templates name a word and the sheet's settings, and fold a sentence the format needs no row for into the row it belongs to by a template's own role, so a sentence sheet's plan is its conversion's. The kit: `TaskSettings` holds a group per word named as it (`highlight.style`, `indicate.size`, `use.trigger`, `button.when`, `travel.ring`, `picture.until` ...; a row's setting `x` resolves as `<word>.x`); builders merged into their word (`turn_on` into `highlight` `style=steady`; `place` into `use` `trigger=near`, a timed press `trigger=hold` with `hold`; `pause`, `goto`, `exit`, `option`, `quiz` into `button` by its To; `number_pad` into `answer`; `marker` into `travel`'s `ring`), renamed (`fade_in`/`fade_out` to `show`/`hide`, `set_state` to `set`, `teleport` to `travel`, `pip` to `picture`, `checklist`/`check` to `list`/`tick`), the rest deleted (`hold`, `placard`, `buttons`); traits and the gate are a row's (`TaskBehaviors.Traits(action, step)`, `ITaskRowTraits`, `IStepGate.Gates`); trait `Teleports` is `Travels`; `TaskNames.pip` is `picture`; `TaskKit.PipName`, `PipAnchor`, `ChecklistName`, `Correct` and the enums `Showing`, `CaptionFrom`, `AnswerKind`, `PauseWhen` are removed (`Moment`, `IndicateStyle` new; `UseTrigger` is Select, Hold, Near). A pointer's own helper is `POINT_<step>_<target>` and the reading's name rule `point`. Spot keys are `travel/<step>`, `picture/<step>`. Settings files and manifests saved under the old names are not migrated. The Tasks sample is written in cells.
  - Test(s): `test_sheet_spec.py` holds every word to a kit builder and every setting to its field in the word's group; `test_sheet_cells.py` holds a sentence sheet's plan to its conversion's (the old sample, and a fixture of every fold); every sheet suite and the kit's Edit Mode tests on the new words (Edit Mode not run here: compiled with Roslyn against the untouched tree's errors, none new naming the package's own symbols).
- **2026-11-27 -- The source scans read what a commit would hold (`tests/test_base_test.py` `_source_files`).** They walked the disk minus `temp_tests`, so once the integration suites' projects moved to `tests/temp_unity` the hygiene scan read a live project's `Library/PackageCache` (Unity's own UGUI source) and failed the receipt; the files are git's tracked and new, not ignored ones (the walk stays for a checkout with no repository).
  - Test(s): `test_base_test.SourceFilesTest.test_a_file_git_ignores_is_no_source_and_the_packages_own_is` (red first).
- **2026-11-27 -- A test's temporary folder is handed out in its canonical spelling (`tests/base_test.py` `create_temp_dir`).** Sheet settings and a sheet's sync resolve the paths they read, which on Windows expands an 8.3 name; a CI runner's TEMP is one (`RUNNER~1`), so three tests expecting the folder as spelt failed there alone.
  - Test(s): `test_sheet_settings.SheetSettingsTestCase.test_the_settings_read_their_files_beside_them`, `test_sheet_sync.SheetSyncTestCase` (2), each red then green under a `PYTHONTK_TEMP_ROOT` spelt in 8.3 names.
- **2026-11-27 -- A test's relative-link project is made in a folder its store makes (`tests/test_unity_host.py`).** It minted its folder inside `tests/temp_tests`, which only the integration suite's projects had made before they moved to `tests/temp_unity`, so a fresh checkout (CI) failed it with FileNotFoundError; a scoped `ptk.TempArtifacts` there now makes the folder and removes it.
  - Test(s): `test_unity_host.TestUnityHost.test_a_link_elsewhere_or_another_version_is_no_kernel`, run with no scratch folder present.
- **2026-11-27 -- The shadow-plane cache's atlas goes with the last plane it served (`Features/ShadowPlane/Runtime/ShadowPlaneCache.cs` `Release`).** Its atlas, command buffer and per-camera listening were freed only before a script reload, so a scene unloaded (or every plane switched off) left a player holding the atlas and listening to each camera for nothing; the last plane's release now frees them, and the next caching plane makes an atlas again.
  - Test(s): `ShadowPlaneActionsTests.The_cache_atlas_and_its_listening_go_with_the_last_plane_it_holds`.
- **2026-11-27 -- A Blended twin of Built-in's Default-Material is made once: a build no longer makes it again (`Kernel/Runtime/MaterialWriter.cs` `Twin`, `Write`).** Default-Material ships RealtimeEmissive | EmissiveIsBlack with no `_EMISSION`, which the editor's validation of a material asset (Standard's inspector, on the asset's first property read) turns into emission on, for the kept twin and for Default-Material itself, never for a copy in memory; so a twin copying those flags matched its kept self only by what had been read before, and "a second build changes nothing" failed by test order. A twin that adds no emission to an original showing none now states it off as a write does (its keyword off, EmissiveIsBlack alone), the state validation keeps; a table made before is remade once.
  - Test(s): `MaterialWriterTests.A_twin_is_one_whether_the_editor_validated_its_original_or_not_and_its_validation_keeps_it`, `RenderEffectsActionsTests.A_twin_made_once_stays_made_though_the_editor_validates_it_on_a_read`; the five `TaskBuildTests` that failed by order, passing in a filtered Built-in run.
- **2026-11-26 -- Manifests: a field of view set on a picture's own camera is kept as its row's by the next build (`Features/Manifests/Editor/ManifestsActions.cs` `Bounds`).** A build asked the picture's builder about its rows before the scene held its plan (`KeepChanged`, `BoundProperties` run before `Build` sets it), so `PipBehavior.Bound` found no root and the change was lost; `Bounds` now gives the scene the plan it walks and each row's place.
  - Test(s): `TaskBuildTests.A_pictures_field_of_view_set_on_its_own_camera_is_its_rows_kept_into_a_new_scene`.
- **2026-11-26 -- Manifests: Place Again on one spot no longer has every spot after it placed anew too (`Kits/Tasks/Editor/TaskBuild.cs` `PlaceAgain`; `ManifestsActions.PlaceAgain`).** Once the part's own `PlacedSpot` was destroyed, Unity's fake null read the part as none, which means every part; the part's node is read once, before anything is destroyed, and a part a kit already took off is not handed to the next kit.
  - Test(s): `TaskBuildTests.Place_again_drops_a_moved_spots_keep_and_its_first_placement_and_the_next_build_searches_anew`.
- **2026-11-26 -- A sample's delivery checks out LF (`.gitattributes`: `com.m3trik.unitytk/Samples~/*/*`).** The Tasks sheet and plan are held to their recipe's output by their bytes. With `core.autocrlf=true` (Windows, GitHub's runners) a checkout gave them CRLF (34 and 505 CRs) and `sync_unity_samples.py --check` reported drift.
  - Test(s): m3trik `test_sync_unity_samples.py` `test_a_checkout_keeps_every_delivery_file_held_by_its_bytes_lf`.
- **2026-11-26 -- The editor's icon copies follow uitk's retirement of seven icons (`Kernel/Editor/Icons`).** `collapse_branch`, `collapse_end`, `expand_branch`, `expand_end`, `tree_branch`, `tree_end` and `tree_vertical` (with their `.meta` files) go, as uitk's tree draws its own hierarchy: the package carries 147 icons.
  - Test(s): m3trik `test_sync_unity_icons.py` `test_the_copy_is_current`; `sync_unity_icons.py --check`.
- **2026-11-26 -- `EffectRecipeDefaults.g.cs` (generated) drops `PulseLeadIn`, `PulseLeadOut` and `AudioSnap`, which nothing in the package read.** The class is `internal`.
- **2026-11-26 -- The indicator glow follows the viewer's rule (`IndicatorGlow.Place`, `IndicatorGlow.Sphere`, `IRenderPipelineHost.SetAdditive`).** A light's size is its mesh's bounding sphere times its largest scale (it was its world box's half-diagonal, up to sqrt(3) larger), the card is added over the scene where the host has an additive switch (URP; Built-in still blends by alpha), its colour reaches the shader encoded as a material's (amber glowed red-orange in a Linear project), and a frame allocates nothing. Each track's card is its own (`IndicatorGlow` is one per track, held by `EmissionTrack`; no Unity object sits in a static), made as its light is first lit and destroyed as the track is switched off, which a scene's unload and a domain reload also send; a glow whose track is destroyed unseen lets go at the next camera. A card that cannot be made is said once however many indicators ask (a missing unlit shader remade a material and leaked a disc every camera, every frame). Breaking: `IRenderPipelineHost` gains `SetAdditive`.
  - Test(s): `IndicatorGlowTests` (8), `RenderPipelineHostsTests.The_built_in_pipeline_has_no_additive_switch_and_leaves_the_material_as_it_was`, `UniversalRenderPipelineHostTests.Its_unlit_shader_adds_its_colour_over_the_scene_as_urps_inspector_sets_additive`, `unitytk/tests/test_indicator_glow_twin.py`.
- **2026-11-26 -- One subscriber to the per-camera render callbacks (Kernel `CameraDrawing`; `PipView`, `IndicatorGlow`, `ShadowPlaneCache`, URP host `Display`).** Four parts each subscribed to the pipelines' callbacks, two never letting go; the kernel now holds them while anything listens (`CameraDrawing.Listeners` counts who), and `Rendering` names each camera a render draws (Built-in: the one starting).
  - Test(s): `CameraDrawingTests`, `PipViewTests.What_it_leaves_out_is_hidden_while_its_own_camera_draws_and_only_then`, `IndicatorGlowTests.An_indicator_listens_for_each_camera_while_switched_on_and_lets_go_as_it_is_switched_off`, `ShadowPlaneActionsTests.The_cache_listens_for_the_frames_cameras_while_it_holds_a_staged_plane_and_a_script_reload_lets_go`, `UniversalRenderPipelineHostTests.A_game_camera_drawn_after_a_display_is_shown_over_every_one_has_post_processing_on_before_it_starts`.
- **2026-11-26 -- A scene's environment has one owner across Lightmap Sets (`LightmapSet`).** Two sets lighting one scene left it on the first set's light whichever released last, a save with two sets saved one set's light, and an active-scene change left the old scene lit and later gave the new one the old one's environment.
  - Test(s): `LightmapSetTests` (3 new).
- **2026-11-26 -- A PiP's camera draws only while its picture is seen (`PipView`).** Folded away it still drew a 640 x 480 view every frame.
  - Test(s): `PipViewTests.Its_camera_draws_only_while_its_picture_is_seen`.
- **2026-11-26 -- A build's PiP spot is marked and taken away once (`PanelsActions.AddShotFrom`, `SetShotFrom`, new `PipSpot`).** A spot that was both the default-named one and the shot's own was planned to go twice but went once, and any child of the part the shot looked from was taken for a build's, a person's locator too.
  - Test(s): `PanelsActionsTests.A_shot_given_its_own_camera_takes_away_the_spot_a_build_made_once_and_never_a_persons`.
- **2026-11-26 -- A clip never waits on a lent holdable that is switched off (`ClipPlayer`).** It never moved on, so the clip and the step waiting on it never ended.
  - Test(s): `ClipPlayerTests.A_holdable_switched_off_is_brought_there_at_once_and_the_clip_plays_to_its_end`.
- **2026-11-26 -- An arrow or ring with no shader to be on is said once (Indicators `PlainMaterial`).** It was made with no material and drawn magenta or not at all, silently.
  - Test(s): `PlainMaterialTests.A_plain_material_with_no_shader_to_be_on_is_none_and_said_once`.
- **2026-11-26 -- One broken save keeper no longer stops the others (`SaveSuspension`, `SceneCapture.View`).** A keeper that threw left the keepers after it saving their edits and those before it never taken again; a failed scene write is taken back on the next tick; an image taken in the call that wrote a prefab takes the write's give-back again first.
  - Test(s): `SaveSuspensionTests` (2 new), `SaveSuspensionTests.A_write_no_hook_follows_is_taken_again_once_the_editor_is_back`.
- **2026-11-26 -- A wired call's number is compared as its mode saves it (`PersistentCalls.Wired`).** 1 or 1.0 against a float call read as not wired and was wired again. A sequence's wiring (`StepsActions.Wire`) asks it too, the last part reading an event's calls itself.
  - Test(s): `PersistentCallsTests` (3 new), `StepsActionsTests.One_event_signals_two_things_once_each`, `An_event_its_owner_does_not_hold_is_raised_and_not_wired`.
- **2026-11-26 -- Overlapping display transforms share a camera's post-processing (URP host `Display`).** Putting the first away turned it off under the second.
  - Test(s): `UniversalRenderPipelineHostTests.A_cameras_post_processing_stays_on_while_any_display_over_it_is_shown`, `Displays_over_every_game_camera_keep_its_post_processing_on_until_the_last_is_put_away_in_either_order`.
- **2026-11-26 -- The runtime contracts ship their stand-ins (`PureStepActor`, `PureCommandSurface`).** `docs/parts/contract.md` asks one beside each contract, with a fixture against it and each provider; `docs/parts/foundation.md` names both pairs. Each stand-in refuses what its providers refuse (`ICommandSurface.Add` declares its exceptions: a command with no label or nothing to run), and `InteractionActor` is held to the stand-in's assertions.
  - Test(s): `StepActorContractTests`, `CommandSurfaceContractTests` (both fixtures), `SequenceDebugCommandsTests.Its_commands_go_on_its_surface_under_their_labels` (on `PureCommandSurface`: shown again, each replaced).
- **2026-11-26 -- Coverage for paths with no test (`Interactions.AddClip` with a window, `PanelsActions.SetFieldOfView`, `MaterialWriter.SwitchEmission`).**
  - Test(s): `InteractionsTests.A_clip_added_over_a_window_given_plays_it_each_side_below_0_found_planned_once`, `PanelsActionsTests.A_spot_frames_with_the_field_of_view_it_is_made_with_and_a_camera_is_set_to_one_once`, `MaterialWriterTests` (2 new).
- **2026-11-26 -- Spots placed as the Shot Manifest places them (`Placer.Stand` `delivery`, ground inferred; `SpotPlacementModel.FloorNear` passes through tops).** Unity kept the ring's first spot where the DCC takes the delivery's outer side, found no spot on a delivery with no floor, and drifted from the reference's floor rule.
  - Test(s): `PlacerTests.A_spot_with_no_side_named_stands_on_the_deliverys_outer_side_and_a_floorless_one_by_sight_as_the_model_places_them`; the golden cases (dotnet).
- **2026-11-26 -- A re-export's staged copy is kept while anything places it (`ManifestsActions.Follow`).** The move deleted the old model that a saved scene, not open, still placed.
  - Test(s): `ManifestsActionsTests.A_move_onto_a_re_export_keeps_the_staged_copy_a_scene_saved_with_it_still_places`.
- **2026-11-26 -- One codec for a manifest's values (`ManifestValues.Text`, `Parse`; colours `r,g,b,a`).** Hex clamped an HDR highlight's intensity; a colour stored as hex still reads.
  - Test(s): `ManifestValuesTests.A_colour_is_kept_as_its_four_numbers_past_1_and_a_sheets_hex_code_still_reads`.
- **2026-11-26 -- The sheet's reader runs with no shell, its outcome read by the contract (`SheetReader.Run(program, args)`, `CommandLine`, `Outcome` returns `Core.Outcome`; `ManifestsActions.Read` keeps severities and handles partial and cancelled runs).** A `{` with no `}` threw, and `%` / `$` in a path broke the command.
  - Test(s): `ManifestsActionsTests.A_readers_outcome_is_said_as_it_is_and_its_arguments_reach_it_as_written`.
- **2026-11-26 -- `ManifestsActions.Problems`, `PlaceAgain(Manifest, IEnumerable<Component>)`; the plan and file digests cached.** One window assessment parsed the plan three times and re-hashed files every time.
- **2026-11-26 -- `Sequence.Enter` is re-entrancy safe.** A move made by a step's exit or entry listener was overwritten: two steps ran at once.
  - Test(s): `SequenceTests.What_a_steps_exit_does_...`, `What_a_steps_entry_does_...`.
- **2026-11-26 -- Tasks kit: task-wide settings offered on no row (teleport, advance, number_pad, pip `Settings`); `turn_on` honours `highlightColor`; a pip's field of view clamped and said; a step's second placed viewpoint keyed `teleport/<step>/<n>` (`VIEW_<step>_<n>`); a marker of an unbuildable teleport held back; a partial build keeps every step's voice line out of the take.** Values set on a row did nothing, a build threw, a moved spot was lost, and a partial build's narration played twice.
  - Test(s): `TaskSettingsTests.What_the_task_sets_once_is_offered_on_no_row_and_what_a_row_reads_is`, `TaskKitTests.A_lamp_turned_on_glows_in_its_rows_colour`, `Two_viewpoints_a_step_places_are_two_spots_each_keeping_its_own_adjustment`, `A_marker_of_a_teleport_that_cannot_be_built_is_unresolved_with_it_and_the_build_goes_on`, `A_build_of_some_steps_leaves_every_built_steps_voice_line_to_its_step`, `TaskBuildTests.Place_again_drops_a_moved_spots_keep_and_its_first_placement_and_the_next_build_searches_anew`, `A_pictures_field_of_view_set_on_its_own_camera_is_its_rows_kept_into_a_new_scene`.
- **2026-11-26 -- The Manifest inspector shows `adjusted` and `moved` read-only; `BehaviorSettingsWindow` fills once and saves on a pause; `TravelTo` gathers bounds once; the kit's enums carry explicit values; the Tasks recipe saves its settings sub-asset itself.**
- **2026-11-26 -- Sheets: a cell names a node exactly, never by its object name (`unitytk/sheets/delivery_index.py` `named` new, `find`, `spelling`, `shortest`; `_cell_reading.py` `exact` new, `_own_helper`; `_row_reading.py` `_exact`, `missing`, `moves`; `_plan_reading.py` upstream; `sheet_check.py` `_states`, `_hand`).** `DOOR` found `CABINET|DOOR_GRP` with nothing said, so a sheet read clean here named nothing in the DCC's Shot Manifest; `where=ARROW1` bound the first of two arrows; a Members name the delivery lacks read clean (or said its take "moves none" of it); a keyed entry named as a cell writes it was missed, so both manifests built it. A cell's name is a node's own name, the end of its path or a declared alias (ignoring case, the name as written winning, another spelling a warning); the object-name tier is the sentence sheets' and the converter's.
  - Test(s): `test_sheet_cells.py` `test_a_cell_names_a_node_as_the_delivery_spells_it_never_by_its_object_name`, `test_an_arrow_two_nodes_share_is_told_apart_by_the_end_of_its_path`, `test_a_members_name_the_delivery_lacks_is_its_cells_issue`; `test_sheet_plan.py` `test_a_cell_rows_keyed_node_is_found_by_the_name_the_sheet_wrote`; the cell fixtures rewritten to node names.
- **2026-11-26 -- Sheets: a setting is read only by the case its row reads as (`_cell_reading.py` `reads` new, `_value`, `read`; `readings/default.json` `use`).** `trigger=hold; hold=2` lost its seconds, `trigger=near; hold=2` its hold, and `when=start` on a button that leaves the task was dropped, each with nothing said. A setting its case does not read is a warning; one written at its declared default reads as left out; `trigger=hold; hold=<s>` is a timed press.
  - Test(s): `test_sheet_cells.py` `test_a_setting_is_read_only_by_the_case_its_row_reads_as`; `test_sheet_spec.py` `test_a_setting_its_rows_case_does_not_read_is_said_never_dropped`.
- **2026-11-26 -- Sheets: a one-node field takes one name, and the reader's messages end in their fix (`_cell_reading.py` `read`).** With `KEY_GRP` and `LID_GRP` made the key the tool and dropped the lid; a teleport with two Targets faced the first.
  - Test(s): `test_a_cell_that_names_one_node_takes_one`, `test_a_word_nothing_builds_and_a_stray_setting_say_their_fix`.
- **2026-11-26 -- Sheets: two targets of a step sharing a name get a helper each, as the DCC names them (`_cell_reading.py` `_helpers`, `_own_helper`; `ptk.ManifestPlacement.helper_names`).** `pip PANEL|LATCH` and `pip DOOR|LATCH` both named `CAM_A01_LATCH`.
  - Test(s): `test_two_targets_of_a_step_sharing_a_name_get_a_helper_each`.
- **2026-11-26 -- Sheets: a project's delivery keeps what its nodes wear and its lights' states (`delivery_index.py` `merged`; `sheet_settings.py` `index`).** `sheet.check settings=`, `sheet.sync` and the Manifest window's read-again never checked a `set` row's state.
  - Test(s): `test_sheet_settings.py` `test_a_projects_delivery_keeps_what_each_node_wears_and_its_lights_states`.
- **2026-11-26 -- Sheets: renamed nodes that go round are refused, and a name is looked up among the nodes sharing its last name (`sheet_settings.py` `aliases` validated; `delivery_index.py` `_lookup`, indexes).** `{"A": "B", "B": "A"}` recursed until Python stopped; 20,000 nodes and 200 rows took 51 s to read and 56 s to check, now 0.4 s and 0.3 s.
  - Test(s): `test_an_alias_cycle_names_nothing`, `test_an_alias_cycle_is_refused_naming_it`, `test_a_name_is_looked_up_among_the_nodes_sharing_its_last_name`.
- **2026-11-26 -- Sheets: the converter writes each cell under the header its template reads it from, and invents no press time (`_cell_writing.py` `_row`, `_FIELDS`; `reading_spec.py` `column_of` new; `sheet_converter.py`).** Its header table restated the formats' headers, held to nothing; a timed press with no seconds was written `hold=1.5`, the kit's default.
  - Test(s): `test_the_cells_are_written_under_the_starters_header`, `test_a_timed_press_its_sheet_gives_no_seconds_is_left_for_a_person`.
- **2026-11-26 -- Sheets: a GLB delivery reads in the plan and the check, and the exit phrase drops "training" (`_plan_reading.py`, `sheet_check.py` `from_file`; `operations.json`; `clauses/exit.json`).**
  - Test(s): `test_a_glb_delivery_is_read_by_the_plan_and_the_check`; `test_an_exit_ends_the_task_or_the_module_never_a_training`.
- **2026-11-26 -- One writer of a Unity asset's settings, and a log's end read without reading the whole log (`project_builder.py` `_set_values` new, `mute`, `tune_pipeline`; `unity_cli.py` `log_tail`; `sheet_settings.py` `load` by `describe()`).**
  - Test(s): `test_unity_cli.py` `test_a_long_logs_end_is_read_without_reading_the_whole_log`; the mute and tune tests, unchanged.
- **2026-11-26 -- A take's window and speed are its row's, never one of its objects' (`Features/Manifests/Editor/ManifestBehaviorAttribute.cs` `RowSettings`, `ObjectSettings`, `IsRowSetting`; `ManifestsActions.cs` `Offered`, `IsOwnRow` new, `SetTune` refuses a row setting for an object, `OwnSettings(behavior)`; `ManifestScene.Settings`, `ManifestValues.Over` (by the row's behavior); `Kits/Tasks/Editor/Behaviors/Clips.cs` `TakeTarget` removed; `UseBehavior`, `PlaceBehavior`, `PlayBehavior` `RowSettings`; `Tools/Manifests/ManifestWindow.cs` Settings > For Its Row..., `BehaviorSettingsWindow.OpenFor(wholeRow)`, `ManifestLines`).** A Use or Place row offered `playSpeed`, `playFrom` and `playUntil` on every object it names, and the build read them for its first object alone. The clip player keeps one entry per take, so they are the row's: offered for the row alone and kept under it (an empty target), as a Play row's are. An object the manifest builds as a row of its own (added there, or given a take of its own) keeps them as its own. An object's own values are read for its row's behavior alone, so a Play row's speed kept for every row of the step no longer reaches a Use row's take.
  - Test(s): `TaskBuildTests.A_rows_take_is_set_on_the_row_never_on_one_of_its_objects_and_reaches_its_clip`; `TaskBuildTests.A_takes_window_and_speed_set_on_the_clip_player_are_its_rows_kept_into_a_new_scene_and_reverted` (kept under the row).
- **2026-11-26 -- Serialized enums keep their numbers (`PipStyle`, `RotateOrder`, `GrabRotation`, `ChoiceOutcome`, `TravelFloor`, `LitAlpha`, the Tasks kit's).** Each member is numbered as it was ordered, so a reorder never changes a saved setting. No data change.
- **2026-11-26 -- The sheet's reader is found as a terminal finds it, a `python.bat` shim too (`Features/Manifests/Editor/SheetReader.cs` `Run`, `Resolve`, `IsBatch`, `BatchLine`).** With no shell, only `python.exe` was looked for, so pyenv-win's shim never ran and the window said no Python answered. The program is now found by PATH and PATHEXT; a batch file runs through `cmd /c` with each argument quoted by cmd's rules (`&`, `^`, `%` and quotes arrive as written), and a cancel stops what it started.
  - Test(s): `ManifestsActionsTests.A_reader_that_is_a_batch_file_on_the_path_is_found_and_handed_its_arguments_as_written`.

  - Test(s): `ManifestSceneTests`.
- **2026-11-26 -- One naming standard: a helper Unity makes for a row that names none is named as every tool names it (`unitytk/sheets/_cell_reading.py` `_helpers`; `Kits/Tasks/Editor/TaskKit.cs` `ViewpointName(step, teleport, settings)`, `Helper`, `Fields.Helpers`; `Behaviors/Spots.cs`, `IndicateBehavior.cs` (`IsArrow` by key), `PipBehavior.cs`, `TeleportBehavior.cs`, `MarkerBehavior.cs`; `Features/Panels/Editor/PanelsActions.cs` `AddShotFrom(..., spot, ctx)`, `SetShotFrom`; `Features/Manifests/Editor/ManifestScene.cs` `Find`; `Features/Indicators/Editor/IndicatorsActions.cs` `PlansArrows`).** The Shot Manifest named a row's own helper `VIEW_/ARROW_/CAM_<step>_<target>`, and Unity named what it made for the same row `VIEW_<step>`, `Indicator <step>`, `PiP <step>`. The reader now writes each placing row's own helpers' names into the plan (`helpers`, by `ptk.ManifestPlacement.helper_name`: one rule, in pythontk alone), and the kit names its viewpoint, arrow and picture camera by them; a build removes the ones it named the old way (an arrow is known by the key it was placed under, whatever its name). Once the delivery brings the helper under that name, a path names the delivery's node, never the object a build added beside it (the row was refused as a tie), and the picture's own camera goes. A sheet in the old sentence format has no Target cell to name from: its rows keep the old names until converted. A dry run that makes or takes away arrows now plans the picture's hidden cues changing with them, as its live run does.

- **2026-11-26 -- A picture of a flat part is framed face-on (`Features/Placement/Model/SpotPlacementModel.cs` `View`, `FaceOn`), pythontk's rule ported.** Among spots that see a part alike, a flat one is framed face-on over the eyes' side, and a spot that sees more of a part always wins; `Placer.View` no longer leaves the face to chance.
  - Test(s): the golden cases (`tests/models_dotnet/SpotPlacementModelTests`), a plate on a wall among them.
- **2026-11-26 -- The Placement feature's search is pythontk's, ported and held to its golden cases (`Features/Placement/Model/` new: `SpotPlacementModel`, `ISpotRays`, `BoxRays`; `Features/Placement/Runtime/Placer.cs` `Stand`, `View`, `Point`, `Seen`, `FloorNear`).** Unity and the DCCs each placed a helper by their own rules, so a camera framed in one tool stood elsewhere in the other. The search is now one model, engine-free (`M3trik.Unitytk.Placement.Model`), pythontk's `SpotPlacement` term for term, held to `ptk.Conformance.cases("spot_placement")` under `dotnet test`; `Placer` keeps its calls and hands the model the scene's colliders. Two changes in what it finds: the search starts on the named side (a spot straight out of the sheet's side is a candidate) and on the eyes' side for a picture; a later candidate must beat the best by a hair, so ties keep the first in every runtime.

The manifest keeps it now (`Manifest.moved`, each by its key, as the Placement feature writes it), listed and reverted with the other adjustments, carried into a new scene; a place that reads as none is said (`unity.task.kept`) and kept as it is. It is said to the builds the kit beside it takes part in, and, where its assembly holds no kit (a project's own script), to every build as before.

It is a camera now, switched off (it never draws to the screen): selected, the editor's preview is the picture, its frustum shows in the scene, and its field of view is its own (the view's as it is made), as with a camera an artist delivered. - **2026-11-26 -- Interaction: where a tool works is an object, and a part put in place stays at its work (`Interactions.AddWorkSpot`, `WorkSpotName`, `MovesIt`, `SetLeaves`, `AddClipTrigger`; `Holdable.StayAtWork`; `ClipPlayer`; `Kits/Tasks/Editor/Behaviors/Spots.cs` `Work`, `Clips.cs`, `PlaceBehavior.cs`).** The real task's screwdriver unscrewed sideways with its middle on the screw: its rig has its pivot at its middle and its length across the screw's axis, and a tool its take does not move stands on its part's pivot, axis on axis (the rig's convention: its pivot at its tip, its axes the part's). That is the task's data, so its answer is a setting, and after a build there was none: the place was the part's own pivot. A trigger now has a spot of its own under the part its take moves (`Work <tool>`), made on that part's pivot and axes (a tool rigged to the convention works as before) and moved and turned like any helper, kept by every build; before a build the tool's fit in the settings file (`extensions.task.tools`) says the same, and the tool's own tip fields say it for every part it works on. Nothing is guessed from shapes. A `place` row's part that its take does not move (the take slides a stand-in home) goes to its spot, rides it into place and stays there out of the hand; it was held, unseen, into the next step. `SetLeaves` no longer asks that the clip move the part.

A spot kept already keeps its file's own numbers, so a scene built anew writes no float noise into the tuning file.

A part the clip does not move, and a carried copy that fades out, are as they were.

A `teleport` row that names its viewpoint (`where`) and what the user is brought to face (Target) now carries the latter (`faces`); the build warns where the viewpoint's forward points away from it (`unity.task.faces_away`, on the row), and never turns the helper for them.

- **2026-11-25 -- What a person changes in the scene on any part a build made is kept, listed and reverted (`Features/Manifests/Editor/ManifestAdjustments.cs`, `ManifestAdjustment.cs`, `ManifestsActions.Adjustments`, `RevertAdjustments`, `Manifest.adjusted`, `ManifestStatus.Adjusted`; `Tools/Manifests/ManifestWindow.cs`, `ManifestLines.cs`).** Measured on a task built from a Maya delivery: of nine kinds of inspector and scene edit only an arrow's size was recorded by the manifest (the one behavior with `IManifestBound`); a page's text and spot and a trigger's press time were put back by the next build with nothing said; a stand-off, a reach and a holdable's travel time survived unknown to the manifest, so nothing could list or revert them and a new scene lost them. Now every build records what it gave each part it holds (the plain fields of its components, in `Manifest.built`); a field that differs is a person's adjustment, kept in `Manifest.adjusted` by the next build, which builds over the parts as it last left them (the builders never see an adjustment, so it is no work for a build, and a sheet's or a setting's later change still reaches the part under it) and puts the adjustment back; its undo restores the person's value. A row holding an adjusted part reads *adjusted here*; the part's row in the window lists each field with what the build gave it and offers **Revert**; **Revert All Edits** and `Revert(manifest)` take them with the edits; **Build in a New Scene** carries them where they are kept. New public names: `ManifestAdjustment`, `ManifestsActions.Adjustments`, `RevertAdjustments`, `ManifestStatus.Adjusted`, `ManifestAssessment.adjusted` (and a row's and a step's), `ManifestWindow.RevertAdjusted`. Also: a manifest asset handed to `Reset` is still its caller's after it (the new scene unloaded it under a script's reference, and the next call read *the manifest names no plan*); one that is gone says it is no longer loaded.
  - Test(s): `TaskBuildTests.What_a_person_changes_on_any_part_a_build_made_is_kept_listed_and_reverted` (every plain field of every part the sample task holds changed at once: kept, a second build changes nothing, carried into a new scene, reverted); `ManifestsActionsTests.A_manifest_asset_handed_to_a_reset_is_still_its_callers_after_it` (red first); the Edit Mode suite.

The words' reading takes a span (`until`: a case's hiding clause, placed where the row's setting says), so `pip ... until=B06` and `teleport ... ring=step` each read as the show and the hide the builders take; `use` reads `trigger=hold`, `hold=<seconds>` and `trigger=near`; a step's own To is carried as the step's `to` field (no builder acts on it yet). `ReadingSpec.helpers` (a viewpoint named by kind) is removed before any release: a teleport's ring is its own setting. **Fixed**: `ManifestsActions.Create` found no model for a plan read against several files (it looked for one asset named by the whole list); the first file is the delivery and each other one an extra. **Rules going forward** sit beside the code: the reader's (`unitytk/sheets/AGENTS.md`: the format is pythontk's, the words' reading only shrinks, exact names, the plan is the contract) and the kit's (`Kits/Tasks/AGENTS.md`: a behavior is a combination of primitives, a setting belongs to its primitive, placing is the Placement feature's and never a behavior's setting, the helper in the scene is the truth).

What restates it is now checked against it: every word has its binding in the reading and the reading binds no other; a binding reads only cells and settings its word declares; every setting a word declares is carried to a builder, or is one of four listed gaps (`highlight` `style` and `color`, the fades' `seconds`, `play` `speed`), so a gap is closed on purpose; every behavior, builder setting and enum value a binding names exists in the Tasks kit's C#; and both starter sheets follow their templates and show every word. A name is found exactly (none, or two, is the issue at its cell, by the sheet's own header), the tool is the row's With (the hand is not followed), and a row's order carries no meaning: a teleport stands at its step's start (or, `when=end`, its end) and a take with `after` follows the take it names. - **2026-11-24 -- Baked Lit is made on import from the project's own URP Lit, each delivery's rule carried by its own reflection probe and a keyword (`Features/Lightmaps/URP/BakedLit.bakedlit` new, `URP/Editor/BakedLitImporter.cs` new, `URP/BakedLit.hlsl`; `URP/BakedLit.shader` and `URP/BakedLitInput.hlsl` removed; `Editor/LightmapsImporter.cs`, `ILightmapRenderers.cs`, `URP/Editor/UniversalLightmapRenderers.cs`; `Kernel/Runtime/IRenderPipelineHost.cs` and its three hosts).** The maintainer's design. `BakedLit.shader` and `BakedLitInput.hlsl` were copies of URP 17.3's `Lit.shader` and `LitInput.hlsl`, pinned to that version by drift tests, made only because the reflection rule's values sat on each material. The values are each delivery's, and each delivery already has its own reflection probe, which Unity hands each renderer per draw: the rule's scale (`envMapIntensity`) is now that probe's intensity (it was forced to 1); the room's light about a surface's normal is read from the same probe for this draw (its blurriest mip toward the normal, through URP's own environment sampling, so Forward+'s atlas and blending hold), where it was seven SH vectors on the material; reflecting by the bake (`reflectionNormalization` lightmapLuma) is a material local keyword (`_UNITYTK_REFLECT_BY_BAKE`); "draw the rule" is gone (a material on Baked Lit has it). The material holds nothing of its own, so Baked Lit ships no more: the URP door's `ScriptedImporter` makes it from `Packages/com.unity.render-pipelines.universal/Shaders/Lit.shader` (`ShaderUtil.CreateShaderAsset`, depending on Lit and the reroute), renamed, its forward pass given the keyword and `BakedLit.hlsl`'s reroute before `LitForwardPass.hlsl`; an anchor it cannot find fails the import, naming the file and URP's version. It imports ahead of the models (measured: a delivery imported first and found none). The model's import depends on it (`DependsOnArtifact`, the path the door names: `ILightmapRenderers.BakedLit`), so no `Shader.Find` null passes silently: a shader not made, a material on no Lit, a remapped project material (now left as it is: it was edited mid-import) and a scale with no probe to ride are each said. Look check (the Lightmaps sample, one camera, before and after, the policy's rule): the reflection moved by a mean 0.054 (max 0.27) of linear luminance on the shadowed third of the surfaces, 0.031 (max 0.18) on the lit third, a mean 11 % overall, darker: the probe's last mip reads the room brighter about the normal than its SH did, so the bake weighs the reflection down further. A level below 1 now bounds the reflection to its probe at that level (it scaled the clamped share).
  - **Removed** (no project uses unitytk yet): `IRenderPipelineHost.SetBaked` (the hosts' one-liners over `RenderPipelineHosts.SwapLit`, now the one swap), `PureRenderPipelineHost.Baked` and `Bakes`, `LightmapsImporter.BakedLitShader` (the door's `BakedLitImporter.ShaderName`), the material properties `_UnitytkReflection` and `_UnitytkProbeSH*`. `LightmapsImporter.VERSION` 8.
  - Test(s): `BakedLitImporterTests` (replaces `BakedLitTests`: made of the installed Lit, its properties and passes Lit's; its forward and shadow-caster passes read Lit's one material buffer layout, compiled; a Lit laid out otherwise refused with its file and version; the door names it, a Lit material takes it with its values; two deliveries keep each its own probe scale and keyword), `LightmapsImporterTests` (the swap and keyword once, a project material left and named, the scale on the probe, the dependency, each refusal said); Lightmaps Edit Mode on URP and Built-in.

  - **The save hook**: `SaveSuspension` takes back exactly what a write gave back, the last first (it scanned again at the end and reversed that), and covers a prefab's write as well as a scene's (`AssetModificationProcessor`: given back before the write, taken again once the editor is back from it); each write asks every one (giving back is idempotent), so a keeper that showed again after a prefab's write is given back for the next scene's save.   - **ShadowPlane**: `Create` asks for its contact and plane by their paths under the target, so two same-named targets' parts never read as one.

A row whose target wears a material an Emission Track with states shows gets an `EmissionStateSwitch` to the row's `state` on that track (`AddStateSwitch`), which its step enables as it starts; the LED keeps the state after the step. `TrackOf(node)` finds the track whose material a renderer at or under the node wears (a render effect's twin counting as it). - **2026-11-24 -- The foundation and the features under the kit, a last review: each fix at its source (`Kernel`, `Contracts`, `Features/{Interaction,Indicators,Viewpoints,AnimationAudio,Steps,Panels,Placement}`, `m3trik/scripts/sync_scene_records.py`).**
  - **A timed press, walked**: `InteractionActor` presses a target whose switched-on trigger plays on a press, held or timed (`ClipTrigger.PlaysOnPress`, `Timed`, `Counting`, new; the actions wire by the same rule), and holds a timed press its time: the walk waits while it counts, a fast walk holds it its time at once (`IStepActor.Busy` says so). It selected such a target, which nothing listens to, and let go on its next move. `SelectNear.Check()` (new) is the per-frame check for whichever hand holds what the target requires.
  - **A clip's end raised**: `ClipPlayer.Advance` takes the layers that ended before it raises any, so a clip ending the frame the one before it starts it again (`then`) still raises its end, its holdables reclaimed.
  - **Back resumes on its own copy**: `SequenceDebugCommands.StepBack` waits for the reloaded scene's own load (by its path) and walks the autopilot among that copy's objects (any scene's load ended the wait, and it took the first autopilot anywhere, an additive start over's old copy among them). With none in the scene, `SequenceAutopilot.Sequence` and the commands' `Sequence` and `Autopilot` look again only once switched on (they searched the scene on every access).
  - **Plans**: a facing viewpoint is named for its faced object's path (`ViewpointsActions.FacingName`: `<viewpoint>_FACING_<path>`, `|` between its parts), so `A/SCREW` and `B/SCREW` get one each (the second took the first's). An authored arrow's hide and a planned part's switch-off are planned once (`ctx.PlanChange`), and a part, a required tool and a faced object are named by their path in a plan's text. `Placements.Place` asks only for what changes (it planned a move for a node already there).
  - **One reader, one rule, one pose**: `PersistentCalls.Wired(owner, when, to, method, argument, ctx)` (Kernel, new) is how `Interactions`, `PanelsActions` and the XRI door ask whether a call is wired; `MaterialWriter.SwitchEmission(material, table, mapped)` (new) is the emission switch of the writer, a twin and a plain glowing part alike; the three windows take one pose beside the eyes (`WindowFrame`), so the debug panel and the picture come up ahead of eyes looking straight down or up too. `SceneBounds.Renderers(node, into, includeInactive, skip)` (new): `Indicator` and `Marker` place themselves by their target alone, never by another cue's arrow or ring on it.
  - **Indicators**: a part's plain material is a new one on the pipeline host's Lit (it was read off a primitive made for it, in a player too, where a primitive's is not the pipeline's).
  - **Panels**: the picture is the size its fields say (`pictureHeight` in `resolution`'s shape, `PipView.PictureWidth`, new; `Panels.uss` sizes no `.pip` or `.pip-image`), so where it comes up before its card lays out is where it stays. `pixelsPerMetre` stays each window's field (Unity keeps a panel settings' pixels per unit internal), held to `WorldPanel.asset` by a test. A document a person adds is sized to its content, as a made one is (Unity's default is a fixed 1920 by 1080); a window made where a document stands asks before changing it, so undo gives its settings back.
  - **AnimationAudio**: `AddSoundCue` leaves the source to the cue, which sets it up as it plays and keeps it from playing as the scene starts.
  - **Contracts**: `HandoffBlock` and `HandoffRendering` sit in `M3trik.Unitytk.Contracts`, as a new type there does; `sync_scene_records.py` takes a record type's namespace from its hand-written half (`cs_namespace`).
  - **Changed or removed** (no project uses unitytk yet): `PointerActions.SetUpPointer(camera, assist, ctx)` returns nothing (its bool was always true); `TravelTo.CentreOf` removed (`SceneBounds.Of(node, includeDisabled: true).center`).
  - Test(s): red first: `InteractionActorTests` (`A_press_held_plays_while_it_is_held_and_is_let_go_on_the_next_move`, `A_timed_press_is_held_its_time_before_it_is_let_go_or_at_once_on_a_fast_walk`), `ClipPlayerTests.A_clip_ending_as_the_one_before_it_names_it_to_follow_still_raises_its_end`, `ViewpointsActionsTests.Two_faced_objects_of_one_name_get_a_facing_viewpoint_each_as_planned`, `InteractionsTests.Two_same_named_parts_switched_off_on_take_and_two_same_named_tools_required_are_each_planned_apart`, `IndicatorsActionsTests.An_authored_arrow_is_shown_in_place_hidden_until_then_and_undone` (a repeat call), `IndicatorTests.Two_cues_on_one_target_place_themselves_by_the_target_alone_never_by_each_others_parts`, `PlacardTests.Every_window_comes_up_ahead_of_eyes_looking_straight_down_or_up`, `PipViewTests.Its_picture_is_the_size_its_fields_say_its_caption_wrapping_under_it`, `PanelsActionsTests` (`A_window_made_where_a_document_stands_asks_before_putting_it_in_world_space_and_is_undone`, `Every_windows_pixels_to_the_metre_are_the_packages_world_panels`), `PlacementsTests.A_spot_placed_anew_where_it_stands_asks_only_what_changes`, `SequenceDebugCommandsTests` (`Back_resumes_on_the_reloaded_scenes_load_alone_with_the_autopilot_among_its_own_objects`, `A_scene_part_none_names_is_looked_up_once_none_is_found_until_its_owner_is_switched_on_again`), `AnimationAudioActionsTests.A_sound_cue_switched_on_plays_its_sound_and_switched_off_stops_it`, `test_sync_scene_records.py` `test_a_record_type_sits_in_the_namespace_of_its_hand_written_half`.
- **2026-11-24 -- The sheet reader, the Manifests feature and the Tasks kit, a last review: each fix at its source (`unitytk.sheets`, `Features/Manifests`, `Tools/Manifests`, `Kits/Tasks`, `m3trik/scripts/check_api_surface.py`, the Tasks sample's recipe).**
  - **The sheet reader**: the clause book has no second tier but a project's folder: `ClauseBook.load_templates(folder)` reads the built-in folder and the project's over it through `ClauseSpec`, and writes nothing (a `TemplateSet`'s writable tier was the package's own folder with no project, which its store wrote its folder marker into and reported as "user"); **removed**: `ClauseBook.templates`. The docs name a project's `clauses/` folder (`SheetSettings.clauses`). The words by which a phrase means what its step shows are vocabulary (`referents`, `NodeMatcher.REFERENTS`; the referent tier read "highlight" in code), naming no part; a matcher builds its word sets once from the reading's and its own (it shadowed the class's lists). A delivery whose keys cannot be read says so in its plan (`DeliveryIndex.issues`, new, kept by `merged` and its copies; "the delivery's keys cannot be read (...): what each take moves and where it ends are unchecked"), where it read as moving nothing. `SheetPlan.digest(path)` (new, public) is the one file digest the plan's source records (was the private `_digest`, read from two other modules). `check_api_surface.py --floors` says exactly what it cannot see: the members of a value handed in (a parameter, an attribute, an item of what a call returned), its OK line too. The Tasks sample's recipe stamps its `animation` record's version from `SceneRecords` (it wrote 3).
  - **Manifests**: `Stale` says what it could not tell (a file another program holds, a settings file this editor cannot read: not JSON, another version), where both read as "not stale"; it compares a newer delivery file with its staged copy by length, then by digests read as streams (it read both whole, twice, every assessment). A manifest's Build saves its scene only where the run changed something, so a second build plans nothing (it planned the save). A switch's value is `true` or `false` (`bool.TryParse`); any other text is refused, where it read as off. New: `SheetPlan.Row(plan, step, place)` (the window and the actions read a sheet's row through it), `ManifestsActions.PlanStem(manifest)` (the kit's tuning file is named from it).
  - **The Manifest window**: the notices offer no Setup of their own (the toolbar's is the one): a plan refusal offers Copy the Command where the manifest says where its sheet's settings are, and one naming no delivery says to set it in Setup. Revert offers what `Revert` drops, an object's own values too (it counted the edits alone). A notice says what staleness could not tell.
  - **The Tasks kit**: a scene declares a rig of its own by one that travels (a `Traveler`), which a build leaves as it is; any main camera that does not travel is made the user, wherever it sits (a camera under a grouping root got no rig). **Removed**: `TaskRig.Auto` (keyed off a camera's parent) and `TaskKit.RigCode`; `TaskSettings.rig` is `Desktop` by default. The tool fits are read once a run, as it begins (`TaskState.Tools`; `TaskKit.Travels(ManifestScene scene, string path)`, was `(plan, path)`, read them again per `use` row, a bad fit raised to the Console each time). A step holds its Next back only for an acting row the run builds (assessed ready), as its dry run plans it (the live run asked the built step).
  - Skipped, with reasons: the build's record is still read from the parts it built (a record made from the settings would restate which steps and rows a run builds and assume each part holds its setting unchanged); the list items are split by both readers (a list behavior declaring `label` among its fields lets a person set it in the manifest, past the sheet's reader); the window still takes a change within 0.3 s of its own run as its run's (no test can show a call deferred past the run comes after Unity publishes the run's changes: the batch-mode editor published them and never ran the deferred call; said in the code).
  - Test(s): red first: `test_sheet_plan.py` `test_no_project_has_no_second_tier_and_nothing_is_written`, `test_a_readings_referent_words_replace_the_matchers_own`, `test_a_delivery_whose_keys_cannot_be_read_says_so_in_its_plan`, `test_the_digest_of_a_fixed_folder_is_the_golden_one` (the C# staleness test holds the same golden digest); `test_check_api_surface.py` `test_a_value_handed_in_is_not_traced_and_the_ok_line_says_so`; `ManifestsActionsTests.A_settings_file_this_editor_cannot_read_leaves_the_rest_unchecked_and_says_so`, `A_delivery_file_newer_than_its_staged_copy_is_a_change_only_where_its_bytes_differ`, `A_reset_asks_before_saving_...` (a second build plans nothing), `A_manifest_says_when_...` (a held file said); `ManifestValuesTests.A_switch_takes_true_or_false_and_refuses_any_other_text`; `UseBehaviorTests.A_tool_fit_that_cannot_be_read_is_said_once_to_the_run_however_many_rows_use_the_tool`; `TaskKitTests.A_step_whose_acting_row_is_held_back_plans_its_next_button_as_its_run_builds_it`; `TaskSettingsTests.A_main_camera_that_does_not_travel_is_made_the_user_wherever_it_sits`.
- **2026-11-24 -- Render effects join the one save hook, and show again after a save (`Features/RenderEffects/Editor/RenderEffectsPreview.cs` `Saves`).** The preview's own `sceneSaving` handler is gone: it is a keeper of the kernel's `SaveSuspension`, as the rig and shadow-plane previews are. Before a scene is written every held effect is given back (the scene saves the renderers' materials as authored); once it is written, each effect that showed shows again, where before a save left every preview released until the effect was next touched.
  - Test(s): `RenderEffectsPreviewTests.A_scene_saved_while_an_effect_shows_holds_only_the_originals` (the saved scene references the original alone; after the save the effect is on its twin and hidden again); RenderEffects Edit Mode 59 passed in the owned project.
- **2026-11-24 -- One save hook for every part, each delivery's materials draw their own reflection rule, a project's renderer settings are named where any project meets them, and a delivery's maps keep the project's cap unless asked (`Kernel/Runtime/ISaveSuspended.cs`, `Kernel/Editor/SaveSuspension.cs` new, `Features/Lightmaps` and its URP door, `Features/Looks`, `Features/Materials`, the articulated rig's and shadow plane's previews, `unitytk.project_builder`).**
  - **One save hook**: before Unity writes a scene, every component implementing `ISaveSuspended` (`Suspend()`, `Resume()`) on an active object of the open scenes, then every editor keeper handed to `SaveSuspension.Add(keeper)`, gives back what a scene would save of it, and takes it again once the scene is written, the last first. `LightmapSet` and `SceneLook` implement it (their `Suspend` / `Resume` are the interface's); `LightmapOwner`'s save hooks and `SceneLookSaving` are removed (`SceneLookPreviewScenes` keeps the preview-scene answer). The articulated rig's and the shadow plane's previews join as keepers, each shown again once a save is written (a shadow plane stopped showing after a save).
  - **A rule switched off gives back at once**: unticking a bound `LightmapSet`'s `lightsOff` or `environment` gives back the lights or the environment it held (it waited for the set's release), and a set that stands down in place gives both back.
  - **The reflection rule is each material's**: the import writes the `handoff` record's `lightmappedMaterials` onto each material its maps light, on Baked Lit, as the material's own values (`_UnitytkReflection`, and the room's light as `_UnitytkProbeSHAr` to `_UnitytkProbeSHC`), and only where the record states them: a file with none keeps its materials on Lit. Two sets in one scene no longer overwrite each other's scene-wide values. Baked Lit holds them in its own per-material buffer: `BakedLit.shader` is URP 17.3's `Lit.shader` and `BakedLitInput.hlsl` (new) its `LitInput.hlsl`, each copied but for the lines that name unitytk, every pass reading one layout, so the SRP Batcher still draws it. A Deferred renderer draws Lit's GBuffer pass: none of the rule (the README says so). **Removed**: `LightmapSet.reflectionScale`, `reflectionByBake`, `BakedShader` and `ProbeShader` (the globals). New (internal): `LightmapsImporter.Reflection`, `ReflectionProperty`, `ProbeProperties`, `ShaderCoefficients`; `LightmapSet.Room(float[], out SphericalHarmonicsL2)`.
  - **A project's renderer settings, named at their source**: `LightmapsActions.Validate` names a renderer that reads reflection probes through an atlas under a set's box-projected probe (`ProbeAtlasCode`: URP's Forward+ with probe blending, which breaks the probe into scattered dark texels) and screen-space ambient occlusion over the maps (`OcclusionCode`), each with its fix as an action: `ReadProbesWhole(RenderPipelineAsset pipeline = null, OpContext ctx = null)` (the Forward path) and `SwitchOcclusionOff(pipeline, ctx)`. The URP door's editor part answers for URP (`Features/Lightmaps/URP/Editor`, assembly `M3trik.Unitytk.Lightmaps.URP.Editor`: `UniversalLightmapRenderers`, the provider of the feature's `ILightmapRenderers`). `ProjectBuilder` keeps a project's renderers as its template made them, so the owned project meets what a person's does. **Removed**: `ProjectBuilder.RENDERER_SETTINGS`, `RENDERER_FEATURES_OFF`, `RENDERER_MARK` and `tune_pipeline`'s `renderer_settings` and `features_off`.
  - **Materials**: a delivery's maps keep the project's cap on their first import unless the project asks for their own size (`MaterialsSettings.fullSize`, off: Project Settings > unitytk > Materials, saved in `ProjectSettings/UnitytkMaterials.asset`). `FixTextureRoles(material, bool fullSize = false, ctx)` (was true). The cap that counts as no one's choice is the project's default, its default texture import preset's, else Unity's (`MaterialsTextureRoles.DefaultCap`); **removed**: `MaterialsTextureRoles.UnityDefaultCap`.
  - **Looks**: `LooksActions.SetLook(root, toneMapping, exposure, Texture2D table = null, float span = LookCurve.Brightest, DisplayOptions options = null, OpContext ctx = null)` sets a look's table, span, cameras, weight and quieted effects; a table wins over the tone mapping (kept behind it), and the plan says so. `LooksImporter` reads the record through `HandoffBlock.States`, `Text` and `Number`.
  - Test(s): red first: `LightmapSetTests.A_rule_switched_off_on_a_bound_set_gives_back_what_it_holds_at_once`, `A_set_that_stands_down_in_place_gives_its_rules_back`; `BakedLitTests.Each_deliverys_materials_hold_their_own_rule_whichever_set_binds`, `Its_shader_is_URPs_Lit_as_installed_but_for_its_own_lines`, `Its_material_input_is_URPs_LitInput_as_installed_but_for_its_own_lines`; `UniversalLightmapRenderersTests` (3); `LightmapsImporterTests.Its_surfaces_reflection_rule_is_the_handoffs_and_none_where_it_states_none`; `MaterialsTextureRolesTests.A_maps_first_import_keeps_the_projects_cap_unless_the_project_asks_for_full_size`, `MaterialsActionsTests.The_default_cap_is_the_projects_own_preset_where_it_has_one`; `LooksActionsTests.A_look_takes_its_table_span_cameras_and_weight_and_its_table_wins`; `ShadowPlaneActionsTests.A_saved_scene_holds_the_planes_own_transform_and_the_preview_places_it_again`; Play Mode `FadeToPlayModeTests`, `HighlightPlayModeTests` (each clock under a paused time scale); `test_project_builder.py` `test_its_urp_renderers_keep_the_templates_settings`; the set's, the look's and the rig's save tests read what the file holds; Validate's scene tests ask no pipeline (`Validate(null, ctx)`).
- **2026-11-24 -- The sheet reader, the Manifests feature and the Tasks kit, reviewed: what a plan was read from is told whole, a dry run plans what its live run does, and the kit is editor composition alone (`unitytk.sheets`, `Features/Manifests`, `Tools/Manifests`, `Kits/Tasks`, `Samples~/Tasks`).**
  - **The sheet reader**: with no project folder, the clause templates are the built-in ones alone (`ClauseBook.templates(None)` gave a per-user folder that shadowed them). `ClauseBook.digest` is the digest of the project's own template files (a line per JSON file, its name and its own SHA-256, CRLF read as LF; empty for none), and `ClauseBook.folder` the folder it read; `PlanSource.clauses` (new) names that folder as the settings file does, so `ManifestsActions.Stale` says a plan is stale when a project's clause template changed. `SheetConverter.convert` lists each value the target format has no column for in `left` ("the format has no hint column"), and moves a hint line to the page only where the format has one. The reading knows the project's retired step IDs (`SheetPlan.from_sheet(retired=)`, `SheetSettings.plan` passes its own): a jump to one is the row's issue, "the jump goes to step X, which is retired", and the check no longer reads another part's message text. `ReadingSpec.restart` (new, on by default) says a jump to the sheet's first step starts over; the reading marks such a row `PlanAction.restart` (new), which the check and the kit's `goto` read (both hard-coded the first step). The contract text names no kit or producer (`SheetPlan.g.cs`, `SheetSettings.g.cs` regenerated).
  - **Manifests**: a dry Build keeps a person's change to a part as its live run does (`ManifestScene.Planned`, read over the manifest's own values), so it no longer plans sizing back what a person resized; `AddSettings` registers the settings it makes for undo; a `[ManifestSceneSetup(typeof(X))]` whose X is no `IManifestKit` is raised; `SyncCommand(string settings, string project, string folder = "")` (new) spells the staging command the window's help shows; the sheet owner's issues report leaves out rows the project added (listed under what it builds otherwise). Window: Build in a New Scene leaves the locked-step refusal to `ManifestsActions.Reset`; the settings window of one object builds its working copy again after a script reload (`BehaviorSettingsWindow.OpenFor` takes no settings type: its behavior's, `ManifestBehaviors.SettingsOf`).
  - **The Tasks kit**: a dry run makes nothing (`TaskKit.MakeRig` plans the camera it would make and what goes on it; `TaskState.Rig`, a planned object, replaces `RigMade`); a rig made starts before the first viewpoint by the Viewpoints feature's own move, planned word for word. The settings decide the rig and the debug tools for any build, a script's too (`TaskSettings.rig`, `debugTools`); `TaskSettings.nearClip` (new, `Interactions.HeldNearClip`); `pipAnchorPattern` is empty by default; a pattern setting that is no regular expression is raised once, naming it (`TaskKit.PatternCode`, `TaskKit.Pattern`), and matches nothing. `side_of` is among the rows' node fields; `TaskBehaviorAttribute.Advances` is `EndsStep` (the clause role `advances` means another thing: the step waits on the user); a teleport plans its spot once (`Spots.Viewpoint`'s); each switching a step does names the part by its object, made or planned (`TaskScene`'s `EnableFrom`, `DisableFrom`, `EnableDuring`, `EnableAtEnd` take a `GameObject` or a `PlannedObject`; removed: their string forms, and `StepsActions`' four string `node` forms, whose name two same-named nodes shared in a dry run). The kit holds no runtime code (`P/AGENTS.md`, `docs/parts/kit.md`; `pyproject.toml` declares no `Kits/*/Runtime` part, so one fails `check_layers.py`). The sample's recipe plans each step through `ctx` (only the new scene and its save are a live run's) and builds in an empty scene, the task's rig its camera.
  - **The Manifest window**: Setup is the toolbar's alone (a notice's duplicated it; a notice offers only what the toolbar does not). A sheet picked where its plan goes (a CSV, in Plan or New...) is refused as a sheet, with the `sheet.sync` command that stages it; every plan refusal names that command (it named a Python call). Test(s): `ManifestsActionsTests.A_sheet_picked_as_a_plan_is_refused_saying_how_to_stage_it`; the notices' buttons have none (the batch-mode editor that runs the tests never updates a window's panel, so a click sent to it is never delivered).
  - Test(s): red first: `test_sheet_plan.py` `test_a_per_user_folder_never_shadows_a_built_in`; `test_sheet_converter.py` `test_what_the_format_has_no_column_for_is_left_with_its_reason`; `test_sheet_settings.py` (the clause folder's digest as a reader makes it again; a retired jump said once, in the plan too); `test_sheet_check.py` (a reading whose first step is no restart); `test_sample_course.py` `test_the_kits_node_fields_are_every_node_the_reading_binds`, `test_the_kits_traits_that_restate_a_clause_role_say_the_same`; `test_check_api_surface.py` `test_a_local_made_by_a_call_on_a_class_reaches_its_members`; `ManifestsActionsTests` (`A_change_made_in_the_scene_...` compares the dry and live builds' whole changes, `An_edited_clause_template_of_the_project_makes_its_plan_stale`, `Keeping_a_kits_settings_in_the_manifest_is_undone_with_what_it_made`); `ManifestLinesTests`, `ManifestBehaviorsTests` (a setup naming no kit); `TaskKitTests.What_the_kit_cannot_fit_place_or_follow_is_raised_by_its_code`, `TaskSettingsTests.A_pattern_setting_that_does_not_parse_is_said_once_naming_it_and_the_build_goes_on`, `TaskSampleTests.The_demo_recipe_plans_what_it_builds_and_a_second_run_is_one_undo_step`; the kit's dry and live equivalence tests compare whole changes.
- **2026-11-24 -- Interaction, Indicators and Viewpoints: a plan names an object by its path, a change by its object's, every dry run plans its live run's changes word for word, a timed press is made and wired once, and the step actor is Interaction's own (`Kernel/Editor/OpContext.cs`, `PlannedObject.cs`, `Features/Interaction`, `Features/Indicators`, `Features/Viewpoints`, `Features/AnimationAudio`).**
  - **The plan key at its source**: `OpContextEdits.Label` and `Key` are always the node's path from its scene's root, as `PlannedObject.Path` is: no scene scan, and a dry run keys an object as its live run does (it used the name unless a live object shared it, so two only-planned objects of one name collided). `OpContext` names a scene object or a component by that label in every ask, so `Planned` and a change or delete tell same-named objects apart. Removed: `PlannedObject.Label` (use `Path`), `Interactions.At`, `IndicatorsActions`' own key helper. New: `PlannedObject(string name, Scene scene)` (a top-level object of that scene; `Scene` says where it is made), `OpContextEdits.PlanChange(ctx, target, action)` (a change planned once, as `PlanCreate` plans a create). `AnimationAudioActions.AddSoundCue` keys its holder and cue by their paths; its docs and `SoundCue`'s name no other feature's concepts. `RenderEffectsActions.KeyPulse`'s planned highlight colour is asked on its node, as its live run asks on the one made.
  - **Dry runs that plan what their live runs do**: `ViewpointsActions.AddTraveler(PlannedObject rig, ctx)`, `GoTo(PlannedObject rig, TravelTo viewpoint, back, ctx)`, `GoTo(PlannedObject rig, PlannedObject viewpoint, back, ctx)` and `Interactions.EquipUser(PlannedObject eyes, nearClip, ctx)` (with `PointerActions.SetUpPointer(PlannedObject, assist, ctx)`, `SetUpLook(PlannedObject, ctx)` for the pointer door) plan a camera the run only planned. `Traveler.Solve(floor, facing, out position, out rotation)` says where `GoTo` puts a rig, so `ViewpointsActions.GoTo` moves nothing in a dry run (it moved the rig and put it back).
  - **A timed press, wired once**: `Interactions.AddClipTrigger(..., whileHeld, float pressSeconds = 0, bool reverseOnRelease = false, startEnabled, ctx)` makes and wires a trigger once, under one plan, and brings one there to play as asked; a clip the player does not play is raised as `MissingPartCode` (nothing made), a tool that is no holdable as `NotHoldableCode` (left out); `PlayAfter` reads a clip a dry run planned as there. `SetPress` and `SetReverseOnRelease` are for a trigger there: a null one throws. The Tasks kit's `Clips.Trigger` passes them in one call.
  - **The step actor is Interaction's**: `InteractionActor` moved from the Tasks kit to `Features/Interaction/Runtime` (namespace `M3trik.Unitytk.Interaction`; GUID kept) with its action `Interactions.AddStepActor(node, ctx)` / `AddStepActor(PlannedObject, ctx)`; it finds the scene's clip players once (it searched every frame). The kit's runtime assembly, left empty, is removed.
  - **Interaction**: a press refused and the selection ending its gesture refuse once by `Target`'s own rule, so `PointerSelect` and `XRSelect` restate none (XR presses, then selects, as a pointer does). `Interactions` reads persistent calls through `PersistentCalls` and keeps the clip window, a clip's motion and the bone boxes in small internal collaborators (`ClipWindow`, `ClipMotion`, `SkinBoxes`); its actions stay the facade.
  - **Indicators and Viewpoints**: `Indicator` destroys the meshes it makes, as `Marker` does. `MaterialWriter.Default`, `Plain` and `Paint` are removed from the Kernel: Indicators' own internal `PlainMaterial`, the pipeline default read once for all an arrow makes. `TravelTo`'s floor on a mesh reads `SceneBounds.TryOfMeshes`.
  - **One scene lookup**: `SceneObjects.FirstEnabled<T>()` (Kernel runtime, new): the loaded scenes' switched-on component of a kind, on an active object and enabled. `Hand.InScene`, `TravelTo.Traveler` and the Panels windows' `InScene` read it (each restated the loop; `WindowFrame.InScene<T>` is removed). Test: `SceneObjectsTests`.
  - Test(s): red first: `PlannedObjectTests.Two_planned_objects_of_one_name_under_different_parents_plan_apart_as_their_live_run_makes_them`, `IndicatorsActionsTests.Two_same_named_indicators_are_styled_and_taken_away_apart_in_one_dry_run`, `InteractionsTests` (`Two_skinned_parts_of_one_name_plan_their_bound_colliders_removal_apart_as_both_are_removed`, `A_timed_press_and_a_press_played_back_are_made_and_wired_once_as_planned`, `A_trigger_for_a_clip_the_player_does_not_play_or_with_a_tool_that_is_no_holdable_is_raised`, `A_user_only_planned_is_equipped_as_its_live_run_equips_the_camera_made`), `ViewpointsActionsTests` (`A_rig_only_planned_gets_its_traveler_and_its_start_as_its_live_run_makes_them`; the stand-at test asks that a dry run moves nothing), `TravelerTests.Where_a_rig_goes_is_solved_moving_nothing_and_its_move_goes_there`, `TargetTests.A_press_refused_and_the_selection_ending_its_gesture_say_so_once_and_the_next_gesture_again`, `IndicatorTests.An_arrow_it_made_flat_or_solid_takes_its_meshes_and_materials_with_it`, `PointerSelectTests.A_camera_only_planned_is_set_up_to_click_and_look_as_its_live_run_sets_up_the_one_made`, `AnimationAudioActionsTests.Two_cues_of_one_name_under_two_holders_are_planned_apart_as_they_are_made`, `InteractionActorTests` (2), `PlainMaterialTests` (moved); the window test's curves are synthetic; the equivalence tests compare whole changes.
- **2026-11-24 -- Steps, Panels and Placement: the two seams a sequence's players meet are contracts, a persistent call is read through its owner, every plan names a part by its path, a rebuilt step's dry run plans its wiring anew, and the debug panel is a command surface itself (`Contracts/IStepActor.cs`, `Contracts/ICommandSurface.cs`, `Kernel/Editor/PersistentCalls.cs` new, `Features/Steps`, `Features/Panels`, `Features/Placement`, the Tasks kit's call sites).**
  - **Contracts**: `IStepActor` and `ICommandSurface` moved from the Steps feature to `Contracts/` (namespace `M3trik.Unitytk.Contracts`; GUIDs kept), so a feature implements one without naming Steps. `DebugPanel` is an `ICommandSurface` itself, found beside the commands by `GetComponent`: the Tasks kit's `DebugPanelSurface` is removed.
  - **One persistent-call reader** (`PersistentCalls.Of(owner, when, ctx)`, `Of(owner, path, ctx)`, `PersistentCalls.Call` with `Target`, `Method`, `Mode`, `Argument`, `Calls(target, method)`; `UnreadableCode`): an event's saved calls read through its owner's `SerializedObject`, each with the argument its mode passes. `StepsActions` read them by re-parsing the event's JSON, and a call it could not read counted as wired; an event its owner holds in no serialized field is now raised and left unwired.
  - **Plan keys**: every part is named by its path from the scene root (`OpContextEdits.Key` / `PlannedObject.Path`); `StepsActions`, `PanelsActions` and `Placements` lose their own key helpers, and a dry run names a planned step, page, shot or node by its path, as its live run names the made one (two objects of one name were planned as one, and a planned part was named by its name alone). New: `StepsActions.PlannedStep(sequence, id)`. The actions that wire an event take the object holding it, never its name, and compute its plan name themselves: `PanelsActions.SayOn(placard, GameObject node, owner, when, text, ctx)` and `RevealOn(placard, page, GameObject node, owner, when, ctx)` (`RevealOn` took no node; `SayOn`'s was a name), `StepsActions.ChooseOn(GameObject sender, owner, when, sequence, ctx)` (was a name), each with a dry-run overload for an object only planned (`SayOn(placard, PlannedObject node, text, ctx)`, `RevealOn(placard, page, PlannedObject node, ctx)`, `ChooseOn(PlannedObject sender, sequence, ctx)`). `SignalOn` names its sender by its path. `EnableFrom`, `DisableFrom`, `EnableDuring` and `EnableAtEnd` take the object a part sits on (`GameObject node`; null now throws, where `EnableDuring` / `EnableAtEnd` wrote "?") or, in a dry run, the object only planned (`EnableDuring<T>(sequence, id, PlannedObject node, ctx)` and the like), and name it by its path in the plan's words; their string forms go once the kit's callers move. New planned-object helpers for those: `PanelsActions.PlannedPage(placard, page)`, `PlannedList(placard, list)`, `PlannedTick(placard, list, item)`.
  - **A rebuilt step's dry run**: after `BuiltFrom` plans to wire a step anew, the actions that follow read it as empty, as its live run finds it (they read its old wiring and planned nothing); a step a dry run only planned reads as empty too, so `SetStarts(..., false)` needs no case of its own.
  - **Steps runtime**: `StepChoice`'s three hidden switches and their load-time conversion are removed (no saved data holds them). `SequenceAutopilot` and `SequenceDebugCommands` look the scene's sequence, autopilot and actor up once and again only once it is gone (they searched the scene every frame). `Sequence.Restart` loads an additive copy asynchronously and unloads the old scene once the copy is loaded (the old one was unloaded before its copy existed, which fails for the only loaded scene). The debug commands' Back waits for a scene load only when the restart loads one.
  - **Panels**: `PanelsActions.AddShot`, `AddPage`, `AddList` and `AddTick` take `bool startEnabled = false` before `ctx`, as other actions do. `DebugPanel.releaseBuilds` defaults to false. The package's theme (`Panels.tss`) holds the panels' look (`Panels.uss`), so every window on the package's panel settings wears it; `look` on `DebugPanel` and `PipView` is a sheet over the theme (empty by default; `PanelDefaults` no longer sets it, nor do `MakePip` and `MakeDebugPanel`). `DebugPanel.InScene` finds only a switched-on panel, as `Placard.InScene` and `PipView.InScene` do (one helper for the three). `PipView.fieldOfView` is kept within 1 to 179 degrees (0 gave NaN).
  - Test(s): `PersistentCallsTests` (3), `DebugPanelTests.It_is_the_command_surface_read_off_its_object`, `StepsActionsTests` (`Two_objects_of_one_name_in_one_step_are_planned_apart`, `Parts_of_two_planned_objects_of_one_name_are_planned_as_their_live_run_switches_them`, `A_rebuilt_steps_dry_run_plans_the_wiring_its_live_run_makes_anew`, `An_event_its_owner_does_not_hold_is_raised_and_not_wired`, `A_choice_event_of_an_object_a_dry_run_only_planned_is_planned_as_its_live_run_wires_it`; the chains compare whole changes), `PanelsActionsTests.Two_placards_pages_of_one_name_are_planned_apart`, `An_event_of_an_object_a_dry_run_only_planned_is_planned_as_its_live_run_wires_it`, `A_part_is_made_switched_on_where_asked`, `SequenceDebugCommandsTests.The_scenes_sequence_and_autopilot_are_kept_once_found_and_found_again_once_gone`, `SequenceAutopilotTests.The_scenes_sequence_is_kept_once_found_and_found_again_once_gone`, `PipViewTests.A_field_of_view_out_of_range_frames_as_the_nearest_in_range`, Play Mode `PlacardSubclassTests.A_subclass_draws_what_the_placard_shows_on_its_own_layout` (replaces the reflection check); `SequenceTests.A_choice_saved_as_three_switches_loads_as_its_outcome` and `DebugPanelSurfaceTests` removed.
- **2026-11-24 -- The editor draws the Shot Manifest's icons (`Kernel/Editor/VectorIcons.cs` `VectorIcons.Names`, `Load`, `Has`, `VectorIcon`; `Kernel/Editor/Icons/*.svg`, copied by the workspace's `sync_unity_icons.py`).** The ecosystem's 147 shared SVG icons sit beside the editor kernel; Unity's built-in SVG importer (6.3+) makes each a UI Toolkit vector image, sharp at any size, and `VectorIcon` (an `Image`) draws one in a tint. A window's status marks and buttons can draw what the Shot Manifest draws instead of text glyphs; the Unity Manifest takes them up once its window is free.
